Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the protocols structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 143. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 917 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the protocols decomposition. By minimality, the element a^143 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 917-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
